# Paper materials link index

Updated 2026-09-07. Each row links the preserved paper directory and a representative Markdown, TeX, and PDF artifact. The five current TPC conversion-batch rows point to source-complete `paper/main.md`; their provenance, page/section map, formula audit, abstract/summary, proof package, and application notes are linked from `TPC_CONVERSION_BATCH_2026-09-07.md`. A dash means that artifact type is absent. For the semantic status rule and aggregate counts, see [PAPER_MATERIALS_INDEX.md](PAPER_MATERIALS_INDEX.md). These links are repository-relative and preserve original files; they do not imply that a mechanical source conversion has independent semantic review.

## External references

External papers cited by TPC are not silently copied into the paper directories. Their URLs, versions, and local applicability notes are recorded in the relevant Bridge-B files and in [REFERENCE_PRIMEGAPS186.md](REFERENCE_PRIMEGAPS186.md). A URL or citation is not treated as a locally verified full-text Markdown conversion.
